Dated this the 01st day of August, 2022
JUDGMENT
 All these batch of cases are filed challenging the order No.
DCKLM/1827/2020-DM2(5) dated 02.07.2021 passed by the Chairman,
District Disaster Management Authority, Kollam. As per the judgment
dated 19.07.2022 in W.P.(C)No.15079/2021 and connected cases, this
Court already quashed the above order. In the light of the same, nothing
survives in these writ petitions.
Therefore, these writ petitions are disposed of in tune with the
judgment dated 19.07.2022 in W.P.(C)No. 15079/2021 and connected
cases.
 Sd/-
 P.V.KUNHIKRISHNAN
 JUDGE
DM
